\section{Cleaning data: Constructing
variables}\label{cleaning-data-constructing-variables}

\subsubsection{Building three time
periods}\label{building-three-time-periods}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{day =}\NormalTok{ DATA) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{period =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(day }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{24} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ day }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{dec25 =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(MONTH }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{12} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ day }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{period1 =}\NormalTok{ period }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ dec25,}
         \AttributeTok{period2 =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(day }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ day }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{electday =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(MONTH }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{12} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ day }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \CommentTok{\#electday = "Election Day"}
         \AttributeTok{protestday =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(MONTH }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{12} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ day }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \CommentTok{\#protestday = "Protest Day"}
         \AttributeTok{period1min =}\NormalTok{ period1 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ electday,}
         \AttributeTok{period2min =}\NormalTok{ period2 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ protestday,}
         \CommentTok{\#period2min = "Post{-}Election"}
         \AttributeTok{period3 =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(day }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{10} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ day }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{26} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ MONTH }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))}
         \CommentTok{\#period3 = "Post{-}Protest"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Building trust measures}\label{building-trust-measures}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{trustarmy =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9a, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9a), }\CommentTok{\#Army}
         \AttributeTok{trustpolice =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9b, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                  \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9b), }\CommentTok{\#Police}
         \AttributeTok{trustfsb =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9c, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                               \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9c), }\CommentTok{\#FSB}
         \AttributeTok{trustcourt =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9d, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                 \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9d), }\CommentTok{\#Court}
         \AttributeTok{trustmungov =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9e, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                  \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9e), }\CommentTok{\#City Gov\textquotesingle{}t}
         \AttributeTok{trustfedgov =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9f, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                  \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9f), }\CommentTok{\#Fed Gov\textquotesingle{}t}
         \AttributeTok{trustduma =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9g, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9g), }\CommentTok{\#Duma}
         \AttributeTok{trustprocuracy =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9h, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                     \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9h), }\CommentTok{\#Procuracy}
         \AttributeTok{trustchurch =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9i, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                                  \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9i), }\CommentTok{\#Church}
         \CommentTok{\#Church was not included in the original paper\textquotesingle{}s analysis, }
         \CommentTok{\#but is included here for subsequent analysis/checks.}
         \AttributeTok{trustun =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(q9j, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }
                              \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ q9j)) }\CommentTok{\#UN}

\CommentTok{\#joining trust measures into gov trust index (trustgovind)}

\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{trustgovind =}\NormalTok{ (trustarmy }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustpolice }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustfsb }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustcourt }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
\NormalTok{                          trustmungov }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustfedgov }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustduma }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                          trustprocuracy)}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\#Gov\textquotesingle{}t Index (as built by Frye and Borisova 2019)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Mapping out/recoding potential
confounders}\label{mapping-outrecoding-potential-confounders}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{age =}\NormalTok{ q1,}
         \AttributeTok{male =}\NormalTok{ q2,}
         \AttributeTok{wealth =}\NormalTok{ q3,}
         \AttributeTok{education =}\NormalTok{ q5,}
         \AttributeTok{permres =}\NormalTok{ q59,}
         \AttributeTok{nationality =}\NormalTok{ q67) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{male =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(male }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{wealth =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(wealth, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ wealth),}
         \AttributeTok{education =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(education, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ education),}
         \AttributeTok{permres =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(permres, }\DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }
                              \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ permres),}
       \AttributeTok{nationality =} \FunctionTok{case\_match}\NormalTok{(nationality, }\DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
                                \DecValTok{6} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{7} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{8} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{9} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{10} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{11} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
                                \DecValTok{99} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ nationality),}
       \AttributeTok{unemp =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(q6 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{11} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ q6 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{12} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ q6 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
       \AttributeTok{statesector =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(q7 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{9} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ q7 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
       \AttributeTok{okrug =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(okrug))}

\FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{okrug)}

\subsubsection{Coding partisanship}\label{coding-partisanship}

\begin{Shaded}

\CommentTok{\#nesting each partisanship variable in its own mutate() function}

\section{Mechanisms}\label{mechanisms}

\subsubsection{Figure 1}\label{figure-1}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{period123 =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(period1min }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                               period2min }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                               period3 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
                               \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{opposition =}\NormalTok{ yab }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ pat }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ sr }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ rc,}
         \AttributeTok{party =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(ur }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                           comp }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                           ldpr }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                           opposition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                           nopart }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                           dk }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{,}
                           \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{partynames =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(party }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"UR"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                                party }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"CPRF"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                                party }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"LDPR"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                                party }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Opposition"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                                party }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Non{-}voters"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                                party }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{6} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Undecided"}\NormalTok{,}
                                \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{partynames =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(partynames)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{trustgovind1 =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(period1min }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{trustgovind2 =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(period2min }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
         \AttributeTok{trustgovind3 =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(period3 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(partynames, period123) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarize}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mean.trust =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(trustgovind, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{                        ur }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ urafterprotest }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ urpostelection }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                        apolitical }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ apolafterprotest }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ apolpostelection }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                        wealth }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ education }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ okrug, }\AttributeTok{data   =}\NormalTok{ data,}
                      \AttributeTok{family =} \FunctionTok{binomial}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{link =} \StringTok{"probit"}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{                        ur }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ urafterprotest }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ urpostelection }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\subsubsection{Check S1: Worst-case imputation: non-responders assigned
trust = 1 (maximum
distrust)}\label{check-s1-worst-case-imputation-non-responders-assigned-trust-1-maximum-distrust}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Imputing NAs with 1}
\NormalTok{data.wc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{trustarmy =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustarmy), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustarmy),}
         \AttributeTok{trustpolice =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustpolice), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustpolice),}
         \AttributeTok{trustfsb =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustfsb), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustfsb),}
         \AttributeTok{trustcourt =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustcourt), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustcourt),}
         \AttributeTok{trustmungov =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustmungov), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustmungov),}
         \AttributeTok{trustfedgov =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustfedgov), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustfedgov),}
         \AttributeTok{trustduma =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustduma), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustduma),}
         \AttributeTok{trustprocuracy =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustprocuracy), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustprocuracy),}
         \AttributeTok{trustun =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustun), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, trustun))}

\CommentTok{\# Recomputing gov trust index using imputed values}
\NormalTok{data.wc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data.wc }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{trustgovind =}\NormalTok{ (trustarmy }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustpolice }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustfsb }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustcourt }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
\NormalTok{                          trustmungov }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustfedgov }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ trustduma }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                          trustprocuracy)}\SpecialCharTok{/}\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Running analysis}
\NormalTok{lm.wc.govindex }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(trustgovind }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ period3 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ period2min }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ protestday }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ electday }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                       wealth }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ education }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ okrug, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ data.wc) }

\NormalTok{lm.wc.army }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(trustarmy }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ period3 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ period2min }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ protestday }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ electday }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                   wealth }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ education }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ okrug, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ data.wc) }

\NormalTok{lm.wc.police }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(trustpolice }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ period3 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ period2min }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ protestday }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ electday }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                     wealth }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ education }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ okrug, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ data.wc) }

\subsubsection{Robustness Check S3: Daily mean trust plot with CI and
loess
smoothing}\label{robustness-check-s3-daily-mean-trust-plot-with-ci-and-loess-smoothing}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Build a single date variable using "MONTH" and "day" variables from original data}
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{response.date =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(MONTH }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{11} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2011{-}11{-}"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\%02d"}\NormalTok{, day))),}
\NormalTok{                                   MONTH }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{12} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2011{-}12{-}"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\%02d"}\NormalTok{, day)))))}

\CommentTok{\# Calculate daily means and 95\% CIs}
\NormalTok{daily.data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(response.date) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{summarize}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mean.trust =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(trustgovind, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{sd.trust =} \FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(trustgovind, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{size.trust =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trustgovind)),}
            \AttributeTok{se.trust =}\NormalTok{ sd.trust }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{sqrt}\NormalTok{(size.trust),}
            \AttributeTok{ci.lower =}\NormalTok{ mean.trust }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FloatTok{1.96} \SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ se.trust,}
            \AttributeTok{ci.upper =}\NormalTok{ mean.trust }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FloatTok{1.96} \SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ se.trust) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}

\CommentTok{\# Plotting daily trust}
\NormalTok{daily.trust.plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(daily.data, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ response.date, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ mean.trust)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_errorbar}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ymin =}\NormalTok{ ci.lower, }\AttributeTok{ymax =}\NormalTok{ ci.upper)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
